Task text mentions <hp:lineSegArray>.\n    # Remove any direct child whose localname matches case-insensitive.\n    for child in list(p):\n        if _localname(child.tag).lower() in {\"linesegarray\", \"lineseg_array\", \"linesegarray\"}:\n            p.remove(child)\n\n\ndef _consolidate_paragraph_text(p: ET.Element, hp_ns: str, new_text: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Set paragraph text to new_text using a single <hp:t> under the first <hp:run>.\n\n    - Keeps the first run (and its attributes) that contains a <hp:t>.\n    - Removes other <hp:t> nodes and run siblings that only contained text.\n    \"\"\"\n    run_tag = f\"{{{hp_ns}}}run\"\n    t_tag = f\"{{{hp_ns}}}t\"\n\n    # Find first <hp:t> and its containing <hp:run>\n    first_t = None\n    first_run = None\n    for run in p.findall(run_tag):\n        t = run.find(t_tag)\n        if t is not None:\n            first_t = t\n            first_run = run\n            break\n\n    # Set new text in the first <hp:t>\n    first_t.text = new_text\n\n    # Build parent map for safe removals\n    parent_map = {c: parent for parent in p.iter() for c in list(parent)}\n\n    # Remove all other <hp:t> descendants\n    for t in list(p.iter(t_tag)):\n        if t is first_t:\n            continue\n        parent = parent_map.get(t)\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(t)\n\n    # Remove other <hp:run> siblings that now have no <hp:t> and no other content\n    for run in list(p.findall(run_tag)):\n        if run is first_run:\n            continue\n        # If run has no children or only whitespace text, drop it\n        if len(list(run)) == 0 and (run.text is None or run.text.strip() == \"\"):\n            p.remove(run)\n            continue\n        # If run has no <hp:t> descendants, keep it (might contain fields/drawings)\n        if run.find(t_tag) is None:\n            continue\n        # Otherwise, if it still contains something unexpected, keep (conservative)\n\n\ndef _update_paragraph_by_replacements(\n    p: ET.Element,\n    hp_ns: str,\n    replacements: list[tuple[str, str]],\n) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Apply replacements to paragraph text; return True if changed.\"\"\"\n    old_text = _paragraph_text(p, hp_ns)\n    if not old_text:\n        return False\n\n    if APPENDIX_SENTENCE in old_text:\n        return False\n\n    new_text = old_text\n    for old, new in replacements:\n        if old and old in new_text:\n            new_text = new_text.replace(old, new)\n\n    if new_text == old_text:\n        return False\n\n    _consolidate_paragraph_text(p, hp_ns, new_text)\n    _remove_linesegarray_if_present(p)\n    return True\n\n\ndef _sorted_followups(csv_path: str) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    with open(csv_path, newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        rows = list(reader)\n\n    def seq_key(r: dict[str, str]) -> int:\n        try:\n            return int(str(r.get(\"sequence\", \"\")).strip())\n        except ValueError:\n            return 10**9\n\n    rows.sort(key=seq_key)\n    return rows\n\n\ndef _find_followup_paragraphs(section_root: ET.Element, hp_ns: str) -> tuple[ET.Element, list[ET.Element]]:\n    \"\"\"Find the existing follow-up paragraphs to replace.\n\n    Strategy (template-aware, conservative):\n      - Look for paragraphs containing the marker substring \"old follow-up\".\n      - Return them in document order under their common parent.\n    \"\"\"\n    p_tag = f\"{{{hp_ns}}}p\"\n\n    candidates: list[ET.Element] = []\n    for p in section_root.iter(p_tag):\n        txt = _paragraph_text(p, hp_ns)\n        if txt and \"old follow-up\" in txt:\n            candidates.append(p)\n\n    if not candidates:\n        return (None, [])  # type: ignore[return-value]\n\n    same_parent = [p for p in candidates if parent_map.get(p) is parent]\n    return (parent, same_parent)\n\n\ndef _replace_followups(section_root: ET.Element, hp_ns: str, followups: list[dict[str, str]]) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Replace the existing follow-up paragraphs in this section; return count modified/created.\"\"\"\n    parent, old_ps = _find_followup_paragraphs(section_root, hp_ns)\n    if parent is None or not old_ps:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"Could not find existing follow-up paragraphs to replace.\")\n\n    # Determine max numeric id for new ones if needed\n    p_tag = f\"{{{hp_ns}}}p\"\n    max_id = -1\n    for p in section_root.iter(p_tag):\n        pid = p.get(\"id\")\n        if pid is None:\n            continue\n        try:\n            max_id = max(max_id, int(pid))\n        except ValueError:\n            continue\n\n    # Build new paragraph elements\n    new_ps: list[ET.Element] = []\n    for row in followups:\n        seq = str(row.get(\"sequence\", \"\")).strip()\n        item = str(row.get(\"item\", \"\")).strip()\n        if not item:\n            continue\n        text = f\"{seq}. {item}\" if seq else item\n\n        p_new = copy.deepcopy(template_p)\n        if max_id >= 0:\n            max_id += 1\n            p_new.set(\"id\", str(max_id))\n        _consolidate_paragraph_text(p_new, hp_ns, text)\n        _remove_linesegarray_if_present(p_new)\n        new_ps.append(p_new)\n\n    #
